2026-10-23 — FIX RUNNER HANGS & ADD TIMEOUT WATCHDOG.
Root cause of runner freezing: (1) Headless Godot runners that encountered unhandled SCRIPT ERRORs aborted their _run()/_init() function before reaching quit(), leaving orphan Godot engine processes running forever. (2) tools/run_all_runners.ps1 lacked a per-runner timeout limit and hung indefinitely when parallel runner slots were exhausted. (3) PowerShell 5.1 Start-Process without -Wait returned null ExitCode for Win32 console executables, misclassifying successful parallel runs as failed. (4) Missing preload of NumberFormatter in resource_icon.gd, recipe_material_tile.gd, island_management_panel.gd, and ui_shell_resources.gd caused parse errors in isolated headless runs. (5) Unchecked accesses to optional shell and panels in feature_navigation.gd and abode_modal_manager.gd caused null-pointer crashes.
Fixes:
- tools/run_all_runners.ps1: Added -TimeoutSeconds parameter (default 45s), implemented recursive Stop-RunnerTree kill logic, and replaced buggy Start-Process with native ProcessStartInfo redirection for robust asynchronous parallel execution with reliable ExitCode capture.
- src/presentation/resource_icon.gd, recipe_material_tile.gd, island_management_panel.gd, ui_shell_resources.gd: Explicitly preloaded NumberFormatter.
- src/presentation/feature_navigation.gd, abode_modal_manager.gd: Added safe null and is_instance_valid guards for shell and feature panels.
- tests/text_transition_runner.gd: Fixed assertion to test correct camera input lock behavior without requiring shell.
Verification: Ran L1 suite (recipe_row, uix_r8, text_transition, hud_readiness) in parallel (exit 0, all 4 passed in <8s without errors or hangs).

2026-10-23 — TIERED VERIFICATION (L1/L2/L3) TO CUT TEST OVERHEAD.
User complaint: ~80% of dev time went to running tests; every small step triggered full regression, full viewport matrix, export and browser checks. Investigation: no rule actually required it (habit only); run_all_runners.ps1 could only run all 99 runners serially; ~54 multi-viewport loops across 43 tests/*.gd were hard-coded.
Changes: (1) tools/run_all_runners.ps1: new -Only (comma/substring filter), -Full (DAO_VIEWPORT_MATRIX=full), -Parallel N (parallel processes; parallel failures auto-retried serially), slowest-8 timing report; default behavior unchanged except single-viewport. (2) tools/apply_viewport_matrix_gate.py (one-shot, idempotent) rewrote 54 loops whose first size is 1280x720 into ([sizes] if OS.get_environment("DAO_VIEWPORT_MATRIX") == "full" else [first]); loops not starting with 1280x720 (portrait/other) left untouched. (3) AGENTS.md new section "分級驗收": L1 affected runners only, L2 domain group + one desktop browser spot check, L3 (full runners + full matrix + export + three-landscape browser) ONLY at large-task/milestone close or on explicit request. [2026-10-03] DEBUG Suite Phase 3: Offline Simulation & Stress Test Sandbox Delivered
- Delivered `src/debug/simulation_sandbox.gd` providing isolated in-memory simulation and extreme stress test capabilities:
  1. `run_offline_projection(session, simulated_seconds)`: Performs zero-pollution offline forward simulation in a cloned GameSession without mutating player progress. Accurately tracks lifespan consumption, training duration, net resource deltas, storage overflow/capping flags, and subsystem resolutions (buff expiration, sect expedition completion, beast/fortune cooldown readiness).
  2. `run_stress_test(session, max_seconds)`: Executes high-volume stress testing up to 30 days (or natural lifespan boundary) and runs post-simulation health checks via `RuntimeInspector.inspect_health` to guarantee numeric stability (no NaN/negatives/overflows).
  3. `format_projection_report(result)`: Generates structured, readable multi-line text diagnostic reports for dev console and UI output.
- Integrated into `src/debug/debug_actions.gd` with two new actions:
  - `sim_offline`: Simulates offline progression (defaults to 24 hours / 86400s) and outputs comprehensive report.
  - `sim_stress`: Performs 30-day extreme stress test and integrity inspection.
- Enhanced `src/presentation/debug_panel.gd` with 2 dedicated buttons: "【沙盒】24h推演預覽" and "【極限】30天壓測自檢" (extended debug action buttons expanded to 28 total).

    - Disables individual MP3 track loop property; connects AudioStreamPlayer finished signal to advance to the next track within the current Era playlist.
    - Automatically updates BGM playlist upon Era breakthrough or reincarnation reset; immediately transitions to a valid Era track if the active track exceeds the new Era boundary.
